The face is evenly lit bright white with no darker, greyer or bluer inner area and no shadow. Keep the small blue bird in the center and the thin ring of tiny blue text close to the outer edge, printed flat and squashed by the perspective. Do not change anything else.

## aio-info-<audience>-<multi|gut|focus|sleep>.jpg, v2 (buy box gallery, slides 2 to 5): new modern pattern, Sofia Pro only
- method (2026-10-16): 1) generate ONE layout with the real bottle (Gemini, 2K, ref = real cutout) until the typography and structure are right; 2) edit it to remove the bottle and the circle (left half empty); 3) for each of the other 7, edit that empty layout changing ONLY the copy and the three icons (ref = the empty layout); 4) composite the REAL high-res bottle cutout and a flat tint circle (dominant label hue at 25% over white) with `tools/place-product.swift` (compiled: `swiftc -O tools/place-product.swift -o /tmp/place`, `place base.jpg cutout.png out.jpg 545 1000 940 880 auto`). The label is pixel exact, which the model could not do (it scrambled NON GMO, the nutrient lists and the seal text).
- structure (all 8 identical): pale #F4F8FF background, 5% safe margin; left: circle + bottle; right: purple tag ('For Kids' / 'For Teens'), two-line headline (navy bold, then purple bold), gray subtitle, three white cards with 1px border and soft-purple icon chips with 2px line icons, two-line FDA legal line, floating navy footer bar. No shadows, no gradients, no serif (never Gelica).
- layout prompt (template): Design a modern, premium square (1:1) product benefit infographic ... all text in a clean geometric sans-serif like Sofia Pro (regular and bold only), absolutely no serif, no Gelica ... (copy per image as in the previous entries, unchanged)
- cost: about $0.05 per generation at 2K, 12 generations including iterations; previous versions kept outside the repo.

## Teens rebuilt around the growth angle (2026-10-17), all new files
Hero: no image, the page uses the video of the Teens Behind the Science landing page (`LANDING.tdk.hero.video`). Every image below is an AI MOCKUP (Gemini `gemini-nano-banana-2.1`).
- `aio-teens-morning|school|dinner|bedtime.jpg` (4:5, 2K then downscaled to 1750 px): scenes with the growth lens (a tall teen eating breakfast beside a door frame with pencil height marks; a girl focused at her desk; a soccer teen at a home cooked family dinner; a teen asleep in bed), each with the real First Day bottle (refs: the real front view of that teen bottle, then the lid and label fix pass of the `firstday-imagegen` recipe 5b).
- `aio-teens-<slot>-before.jpg`: pixel aligned negative twins for the slider (rushed and untouched cereal; drained at the laptop; fast food burger and worried parents; awake and restless in bed), made by editing the fixed photo (remove bottle and gummy, change only the mood).
- `aio-buybox-teens.jpg` (1:1): the tall teen and his mother beside the height marks with the four bottles in front; scene at 1K with the four real fronts as refs, then one 4K label restoration pass, saved at 2048 px.
- `aio-teens-plate.jpg` (1:1): a teen's hand drawing a new pencil height mark on a kitchen door frame. `aio-teens-timeline-1|2|3.jpg` (4:3): breakfast, friends after school, making a fresh sandwich.
- `aio-icon-bones.jpg` (1:1): flat vector icon of a bone beside a growth chart ruler, same recipe as the other `aio-icon-*` files.
- `aio-teens-facts.jpg` and `aio-info-teens-multi.jpg`: the new infographic pattern (see the entry above); the facts image reuses the production teen statements ("More Folate than 3 cups of spinach", "More Vitamin D than 240 mushrooms" and so on) in the new look.

- prompt: Edit image 1 with a strict, minimal edit. Keep everything exactly the same: the same people, room, furniture, objects, composition, crop, zoom, camera angle, colors, lighting, depth of field, expressions, the gummy if one is shown, and the exact position, size, angle and perspective of the First Day bottle, so this image still overlays pixel-aligned with the original. Change only the First Day bottle (Daily Focus Brain Support), in three ways. (1) The lid: the top surface of the lid, wherever it is visible, must be the same bright white with a very faint cool blue-grey tint as the white upper band of the bottle, never green, red, teal, purple, beige, cream or grey. It is a perfectly flat, smooth, solid, closed disc with no rim, no lip, no recess, no hollow, no hole and no groove (image 3 shows the real bottle held in a hand, only as a shape reference for the lid). (2) The lid artwork: a small blue line-art bird exactly in the center of the lid top and a very thin circular line of tiny blue text around its inner edge (image 4 shows the real lid from above, only as a reference for what is printed, ignore its colors, hand and background); keep the strong perspective foreshortening of the lid as seen in image 1, so the bird is a tiny squashed drawing and the ring of text a very thin squashed line, barely legible, subtle and delicate. If the lid top is not visible in image 1, add nothing. (3) The label: image 2 is the real high resolution front view of this exact bottle. Redraw all printed text and graphics on the bottle so they match image 2 exactly, crisp and correctly spelled, following the curve and perspective of the bottle in image 1: the first day logo, the audience word, the NO JUNK seal, the product name, the small ingredient lines, the certification badges and the line 60 GUMMIES | DIETARY SUPPLEMENT. Natural photographic sharpness, absolutely no glow, no halos, no colored outlines, no color fringing, no oversharpening, no change of saturation. Do not change anything else.

### Teens negatives, fourth round (2026-10-17), after the anomaly and logic check was added to the skill
- School negative had a girl with three arms: regenerated 3 variants with an explicit anatomy rule, kept the one with a clear yawn and exactly two arms. Door frame negative: editing the positive never made the teen shorter and left the mother's pencil far above his head (4 variants failed), so it was generated as its own scene in the same kitchen (reference = the positive): the teen's head only reaches the mother's chin, the pencil is at the top of his head, only two faint marks (4 variants, kept `n1`). Dinner pair: a stray extra hand behind the teen's chair (present in both versions) was removed with a local patch (3 variants each, kept v2). Night pair checked, no anomaly.

If the lid top is not visible in image 1, add nothing. (3) The label: image 2 is the real high resolution front view of this exact bottle. Redraw all printed text and graphics on the bottle so they match image 2 exactly, crisp and correctly spelled, following the curve and perspective of the bottle in image 1: the first day logo, the word TODDLERS, the NO JUNK seal, the product name, the small ingredient lines, the certification badges and the line 60 GUMMIES | DIETARY SUPPLEMENT. Natural photographic sharpness, absolutely no glow, no halos, no colored outlines, no color fringing, no oversharpening, no change of saturation. Do not change anything else.

## Toddlers page (2026-10-17), all new files, AI MOCKUPS
Angle from the live page `/pages/5-pediatric-warning-signs-your-toddler-may-have-a-nutrient-gap` ("is it just a phase?"). Different family in every scene: hero = a South Asian father comforting his crying toddler daughter (no bottle); `aio-toddlers-meltdown` = Hispanic mother and toddler boy on the kitchen floor; `aio-toddlers-words` = Black father and toddler girl with a picture book; `aio-toddlers-potty` = East Asian mother and toddler boy in a bathroom; `aio-toddlers-picky` = blonde toddler girl and her mother at a table. Each positive has the real toddler bottle (refs `toddlers-multi.png` or `toddlers-prob.png` from `public/assets/shop`, label and lid fix pass, lid variants through `tools/lid-patch.sh`, meltdown needed the extra no-blue colour rule) and a pixel aligned negative twin (tantrum, tearful and frustrated, tummy ache, refusing the plate) checked for anatomy. `aio-buybox-toddlers.jpg` = a Black father and toddler girl behind the two bottles (1K scene, then one 4K label restoration, saved at 2048 px). `aio-info-toddlers-multi|gut.jpg` and `aio-toddlers-facts.jpg` use the infographic pattern with the real bottle composited by `tools/place-product.swift`. `aio-toddlers-plate.jpg` (hand pushing a plate), `aio-toddlers-timeline-1|2|3.jpg` (breakfast, building blocks, eating vegetables, three different children), `aio-icon-gut.jpg` (flat vector tummy).
